This suggests we can greatly improve by hierarchical Kronecker products using short sparse "tile" sequences. In fact, we can nest: if we have a sequence of length N with low obj, and a short "pruning" sequence c length L small, kron reduces obj by factor obj_c/2 (<1 if obj_c<2). It also multiplies length by L and imposes periodic support/weights pattern of length L (with zeros) scaled by base at block scale. This is like applying a filter to the base: a_{iM+k}=b_i c_k. If c has zeros, many coefficients zero. This may be very effective.

### Algorithm design candidate
Use a sequence of stages:
- Stage 0: Get initial best from height_sequence_1 if valid, plus a few deterministic fallbacks: uniform, cos, polyphase constructors. Evaluate objectives. Keep best.
- Stage 1: Optimize at original length (≤8192) using robust smooth FISTA. Maybe 300-600s. We can do multiple β schedules and restarts. Also exact active polish.
- Stage 2: Upsample/expand to 2x or more using different methods:
  * Linear interpolation (previous)
  * Polyphase upsample: repeat each point with optimized pattern length 2,4 (choose p).
  * Random perturbation/multiscale: Insert zeros? Use learned filter from short benchmark.
  Select candidate with best objective after short low-beta FISTA (warm start). Keep best.
- Stage 3: Continue FISTA at larger n with time-adaptive β and active set, maybe up to 3x or 4x. Ensure final n chosen for evaluation: maybe 16384 or 32768. Also try downsampling final if better.
- Stage 4: LP-based polishing for best length (if n not too large). Solve primal LP max sum b under current M or dual minimize sum u with f*u≥1. Use scipy.optimize.linprog with sparse, or a custom projected gradient on dual? We can implement primal via scipy if n≤4096/8192. But maybe we can implement a simpler "LP step" using only active constraints and a smaller solver? Let's explore.

#### Implementing LP step
We can solve dual: variables u length L=2n-1, minimize sum u, s.t. F u ≥ 1, u≥0, where (F u)_j = ∑_{k} u_k f_{k-j} if valid. This is equivalent to ∑_{k: k-j in [0,n-1]} u_k f_{k-j} ≥1. If f has zeros, columns zero for some j impossible. If f has all positive, feasible. f should have many small positives; but if zeros, dual constraint infeasible because cannot reach ≥1 for zero coords. Could add small epsilon to f? In LP, if f_j=0, impossible; but primal may have support only? Since f feasible? For near optimum likely all positive. We could ensure positivity by adding tiny noise to zero entries. But evaluate allows zeros. For LP use f_pos = f + ε perhaps; then dual constraints approximate. Also variables u length 2n-1 large.

It may be dense. Need active constraints to reduce.

### Robust FISTA improvements
Previous FISTA may stuck due β adaptation. We can design a more principled algorithm:
- Objective H_β(a)=β log(∑ exp((c_k-Mshift)/β)) (log-sum-exp). Convex? H_β of c? c is quadratic nonconvex; but for local optimization. We can minimize with FISTA. Pseudo run_smooth_fista(a_init, time, rng, betas, top_k etc):
```
n = len(a)
a = np.asarray; a=np.clip(a,0,None); a=a/a.sum()
y=a; t=1; beta=beta_init
best_obj=inf; best=a
for iter until time:
   c=conv(y)
   C=c.max()
   obj=2*n*C
   track best y
   stuck = it-last_improve
   # choose k/weights
   if beta < beta_switch or rng:
       weights = softmax(beta*(c-C))
   else:
       idx = top_lags(c, k)
       weights=normalized (possibly equal or beta)
   grad = 2*ifft(fft(weights)*conj(fft_y))? Reuse F? We computed F=fft(y) for conv; we need conj(F). So after c, grad = 2*real(ifft(fft(weights)*conj(F)))[:n].
   gnorm=norm(grad)
   step = step_scale / (beta + gnorm*? )
   x=project(y - step*grad,1)
   t_new...
   y = x + ...
   y = clip0, maybe renormalize if sum not 1 due projection bug.
   if stuck large: noise, restart mom, increase beta, or beta reset.
```

### FISTA function design in detail
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=5000.0,
              mode='lse', step_scale=1.0, topk_frac=1/32, topk_max=2048, use_restarts=True):
    start=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a)
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None).astype(float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-15: a=np.ones(n)*S/n
    else: a*=S/s
    Fsize=scipy.fft.next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; beta=beta_init
    best_conv=conv.max(); best=a.copy()
    last_improve=0; it=0; step_scale_cur=step_scale
    while time-start<max_time:
       it+=1
       beta=min(beta_max, beta* (1+beta_rate))  # or initial*exp
       F=fft(y,Fsize)
       c=real(ifft(F*F))[:2n-1]
       # tiny negative clip? c=max(c,0?)
       cm=c.max()
       if cm < best_conv - 1e-12:
           best_conv=cm; best=y.copy(); last_improve=it; stuck=0
       else:
           stuck=it-last_improve
       # weights
       if mode in ('lse','hybrid') and beta < 1e5 and (mode!='hybrid' or stuck<200 or rng.random()<0.2):
          shifted=beta*(c-cm); shifted-=shifted.max(); e=exp(shifted); sl=e.sum()
          if sl<1e-300:
             w=np.zeros(2n-1); w[argmax(c)]=1
          else: w=e/sl
       else:
          k=max(4, min(topk_max, n//16))
          # increase k if stuck: k = min(topk_max, k*(1+stuck//5000))
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -min(k,len(c)))
          vals=c[idx]
          # use lse on vals with beta_top = max(beta, 10)
          v=vals - vals.max(); e=exp(beta_top*v shifted); if sum... equal
          w=zeros; w[idx]=weights
       g=2*real(ifft(fft(w,Fsize)*np.conj(F)))[:n]
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm<1e-15: break
       # adaptive step
       if mode topk? step=step_scale / (max(beta,1.0))? For LSE.
       step = step_scale / max(beta, 1.0)
       if stuck > 500: step *= max(0.2, 1.0/(1+stuck/2000))
       if stuck > 5000: # noise/restart
           noise_scale = 0.002*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-stuck/5000)
           y = project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0,noise_scale,n), S)
           t=1.0; stuck=0? maybe
       x=project_simplex(y - step*g, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y=np.maximum(y,0)
       a=x; t=t_new
       # Force sum exactly? projection does.
       # symmetrize? maybe periodic outside.
    return best
```
Potential issue: beta increasing multiplicatively by (1+rate) each iter; if 50k iterations, beta huge. Fine.

Need project_simplex exact:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=len(v)
    if S<=0: return zeros
    # if all same? 
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u - (css-S)/np.arange(1,n+1)>0)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: theta=(v.sum()-S)/n; return np.maximum(v-theta,0)?
    theta=(css[rho[-1]]-S)/(rho[-1]+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Using searchsorted? Standard.

Performance: sorting each iter. Could implement simpler for speed? Let's keep.

Maybe use `np.maximum(v,0); s=sum; if s>0 v*=S/s else uniform` as fallback. Exact projection requires sorting. We'll use exact.

But FISTA y after momentum may be negative; projection.

### Neighbor/symmetrize

```
def symmetrize(a):
   n=len(a)
   b=0.5*(a + a[::-1])
   s=b.sum(); if s>0 b*=S/b.sum()
   return b
```

```
def polyphase_upsample(a, c):
   n=len(a); L=len(c); y=np.empty(n*L)
   for j in range(L): y[j::L] = a * c[j]
   return y
```

```
def assemble_polyphase(base, c):
    m=len(base); L=len(c); y=np.empty(m*L)
    for k in range(L): y[k::L]=base*c[k]
```

### Filter factor standalone optimizer
We can use random + FISTA on c to minimize factor. But in program maybe too much; we can include a small `optimize_filter_standalone(L, time)` using similar but no base and objective factor= L*max(folded conv). Could provide good init for filter. But maybe not necessary. We can include known c4.

Standalone objective:
```
def filter_factor(c):
   c=c/sum; q=np.convolve(c,c); folded=zeros(L); folded[:L]+=q[:L]; if L-1: folded[:L-1]+=q[L:-1]? Wait q length 2L-1 indices 0..2L-2. For r=0..L-1, folded[r]=q[r]+(q[r+L] if r+L<2L-1 else0). So q[L:] indices L..2L-2 length L-1 corresponds r0..L-2. folded[:L-1]+=q[L:]
   return L*max(folded)
```
We can optimize via simplex gradient of max folded. Gradient: choose r argmax; folded[r]=q[r]+q[r+L] if. Derivative wrt c_j =? q = c*c; grad for folded at r similar polyphase gradient on a base of length1? Easier finite diff for L≤16. But not needed.

### Implementing `run_fista` robust with time and print
Need print only moderate. Use returns best.

Pseudo code with all top-level:
```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=5000.0, step_scale=1.0, topk_frac_den=32, topk_max=2048, use_noise=True):
    n=...
    if n==0 return
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=... normalize
    fsize=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; beta=beta_init
    c=fft_convolve_self(y); best_c=c.max(); best=y.copy(); last=0
    step_scale_cur=step_scale
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-start<max_time:
       it+=1
       beta=min(beta_max, beta*(1.0+beta_rate))
       F=fft(y,fsize)
       c=np.real(ifft(F*F))[:2*n-1]
       cmax=float(np.max(c))
       if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=cmax; best=y.copy(); last=it; stuck=0
       else:
          stuck=it-last
       # choose top
       size=2*n-1
       use_top = (beta > 400.0) or (stuck > 1000 and (it % 3 == 0) )
       if use_top:
          k=max(4, min(topk_max, n//topk_frac_den))
          if stuck > 2000:
             k=min(topk_max, int(k*(1.0+stuck/5000.0)))
          if k >= size: idx=np.arange(size); vals=c
          else:
             idx=np.argpartition(c, size-k)[size-k:]
             vals=c[idx]
          vmax=vals.max()
          shift=20.0*(vals-vmax)  # moderate
          shift-=shift.max()
          e=np.exp(shift); esum=e.sum()
          wvals=e/esum if esum>0 else np.ones(k)/k
       else:
          shift=beta*(c-cmax); shift-=shift.max(); e=np.exp(shift); esum=e.sum()
          wnp... if top1 if underflow
          if underflow: w=np.zeros(size); w[argmax(c)]=1
          else: w=e/esum
       W=fft(w,fsize)
       g=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(F)))[:n]
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm<1e-15: break
       step=step_scale_cur / max(beta if not use_top else max(20.0, beta*0.1), 1.0)
       # step adjust
       if stuck > 500:
           step *= max(0.25, 1.0/ (1.0 + 0.001*stuck))
           step_scale_cur=max(0.2, step_scale_cur*0.98)
       else:
           step_scale_cur=min(2.0, step_scale_cur*1.001)
       x=project_simplex(y - step*g, S)
       t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y=x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y=np.maximum(y,0)
       # if sum drift
       ys=y.sum()
       if ys>1e-12: y*=S/ys
       else: y=best.copy()
       a=x; t=t_new
       if use_noise and stuck > 4000 and ((it % 400) == 0):
           noise=0.003*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-stuck/8000)
           y=project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0,noise,n), S)
           t=1.0
           beta=20.0
           # do not reset best
    return best, best_c
```
Potential bug: if use_top, `w` length size. If idx unsorted, okay. `np.argpartition(c, size-k)[size-k:]` works if k<size. If k>=size use all.
Weights for topk using β=20; for top1 underflow use argmax. But if use_top and vals many near max, okay.

Step scale update maybe too complicated. Could simplify:
```
eff_beta = max(1.0, beta) if not use_top else max(10.0, beta/20.0)
step = step_scale_cur / eff_beta
if stuck > 1000: step *= max(0.3, 1000.0/(1000.0+stuck))
```
Use that.

When use_top with beta large, eff_beta ~beta/20 could still large step small; maybe set eff_beta = min(max(beta/20,10),100). Good.

Add objective guard: if cmax > best_c + 0.2 and it>50: reduce step_scale, reset momentum. But best_c initial maybe; if initial bad, can. Use if cmax > 2*best_c? no.

Since FISTA may diverge, use damping: if cmax > best_c + 0.1 and stuck>0:
```

```
But y after update not evaluated until next; could keep. We can check if x (not y) conv? Too expensive. Skip.

### Objective eval and compare
Use `compute_objective(a)`:
```
def compute_objective(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15: return inf
    n=len(a)
    c=fft_convolve_self(a)
    return float(2*n*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```

### LP details code
Top-level `lp_polish(a, max_time, max_rows=128)`:
```
def lp_polish(a, max_time, max_rows=128):
   start=time.monotonic(); n=len(a); if n>65536: return a, None
   S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize; c=fft_convolve_self(a); M=max
   size=2*n-1
   r=min(max_rows, size)
   idx=np.argpartition(c, size-r)[size-r:]
   # include argmax? top includes.
   A=np.zeros((r,n), float)
   for t,k in enumerate(idx):
      j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(n-1,k)
      L=j1-j0+1
      A[t,j0:j1+1]=a[k-j1:k-j0+1][::-1]
   # bounds
   maxa=np.max(a)
   U=max(0.05, 10.0*(S/n), 2.0*maxa)
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   try:
      if linprog is None: return a
      sol=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(r,float(M)), bounds=[(0,U)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
   except Exception: return a
   if not sol.success or sol.x is None: return a
   b=sol.x; s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12 return a
   b_scaled=b*(S/s)
   best=a, best_obj=compute_objective(a)
   # try blends
   for t in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
      cand=(1-t)*a + t*b_scaled
      if cand.sum()>0: cand*=S/cand.sum()
      obj=compute_objective(cand)
      if obj<best_obj: best=cand
   # try b_scaled then sym
   return best
```
Buddy: If LP objective lower but b scaled has max convolution maybe large inactive; blend helps.

Could include variable upper U based on `U = max(0.1, 2*np.max(a), 10*S/n)`. If M small, okay.

Need pass max_rows based on n: for n16384 rows=64 to be safe; 32768 rows=32? But LP matrix rows*r n: 64*16384=1M, good. 128*32768=4M, okay. Maybe use 64 for >8192.

Use `bounds=np.array([(0,U),...])` or list. For n=32768 list large but okay.

Potential issue: `A` dense 4M float 32MB, ok. HiGHS may convert to sparse? It may accept dense. Good.

Should we include negative f? clipped positive.

```
def update_best(best_a, best_obj, cand, obj=None):
    if cand is None: return best_a,best_obj
    if obj is None: obj=compute_objective(cand)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj<best_obj:
        return np.asarray(cand,float).copy(), float(obj)
    return best_a,best_obj
```

```
def multiscale_refine(base, target_n, max_time, rng):
   # returns (candidate, obj) best after some filter/full FISTA
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline_local=start+max_time
   best=None; bestobj=inf
```

```
def downsample_average(a, target):
   n=len(a)
   if target<=0: return empty
   if n <= target: return np.interp? maybe upsample linear
   if n % target ==0:
      factor=n//target; return a[:target*factor].reshape(target,factor).mean(axis=1)
   else:
      # take n_use = target*(n//target); if 0 -> target
      # Use linear resize: np.interp from linspace to target, then average? Simpler: blocks with variable length via indices.
      old=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); new=np.linspace(0,1,target,endpoint=False); return np.interp(new,old,a)
```
If not divisible but close, interpolation okay. For factor 2/4 from 8192, divisible. Good.

### Potential performance of project_simplex sorting at each FISTA
We can optimize projection if target S small:
```
def project_simplex(v,S):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   n=len(v)
   if n==0: return v
   if S<=0: return np.zeros(n)
   # Quick if all positive and sum close? But need projection.
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   css=np.cumsum(u)
   idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   theta_arr=(css-S)/idx
   # valid where theta_arr < u (strict)
   valid = u > theta_arr
   if valid.any():
      rho=int(np.max(np.nonzero(valid)[0]))+1
      theta=float((css[rho-1]-S)/rho)
   else:
      theta=float((v.sum()-S)/n)
   return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
This is O(n log n). Good.

Could use `np.maximum(v,0).sum()` if close? Not.

### `multiscale_to_target` implementation details

```
def multiscale_to_target(base, target_n, max_time, rng):
    local_start=time.monotonic(); local_deadline=local_start+max_time
    best=None; best_obj=inf
    base_len=len(base)
    configs=[]
    for L in (4,8,2,16,32):
        if target_n % L ==0:
            m=target_n//L
            if m<=base_len:
                configs.append((L,m))
    # also linear interpolation if target_n <= 2*base_len? just one candidate
    n_configs=len(configs)
    per_filter = min(60, max(20, (local_deadline-time.monotonic())/(max(1,n_configs)*2.0)))
    per_short = min(45, per_filter)
    for idx,(L,m) in enumerate(configs):
       rem=local_deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 30: break
       base_m = downsample_average(base,m) if base_len>=m else upsample_linear(base,m)
       c0=make_filter_init(L)
       fb=min(per_filter, rem*0.4)
       y, _ = optimize_filter_for_base(base_m, L, fb, rng, init_c=c0)
       best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,y)
       if rem > 20:
          sb=min(per_short, rem*0.4)
          y2,_=run_fista(y, sb, rng, beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=3000.0, step_scale=1.0)
          best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,y2)
    # linear candidate
    rem=local_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50 and target_n <= 4*base_len:
       yl=upsample_linear(base,target_n)
       best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,yl)
       sb=min(45, rem*0.3)
       y2,_=run_fista(yl,sb,...)
       best,best_obj=update_best(...)
    return best,best_obj
```
But if base_len > target_n, downsample? For target 16384 base_len8192, okay. For target 32768 base_len16384, okay. If base_len larger than target, we could downsample to target? But we usually target larger. If not, skip.

### `optimize_filter_for_base` return tuple
```
def optimize_filter_for_base(base, L, max_time, rng, init_c=None, beta=20.0):
    m=len(base); n=m*L
    if n==0: return None, inf
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    b=normalize_to_sum(base,1.0)
    c=normalize_to_sum(init_c if init_c is not None else np.ones(L)/L,1.0)
    c_y=c.copy()
    t=1.0
    fsize=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    y=S*assemble_polyphase(b,c_y)
    conv=fft_convolve_self(y); best_c=conv.max(); best_y=y.copy()
    start=...; it=0; stuck=0; last=0; step=0.2
    while time-left>0:
       it+=1
       F=fft(y,fsize)
       cconv=np.real(ifft(F*F))[:2*n-1]
       cm=cconv.max()
       if cm < best_c -1e-12: ...
       else stuck=...
       # weights LSE beta=20; if stuck large topk with L? Since conv length 2n-1 large, use topk if beta >? Keep full LSE? Cost FFT of weights length n. Fine.
       shift=beta*(cconv-cm); ... weights
       gy=2*real(ifft(fft(weights,fsize)*conj(F)))[:n]
       gc=np.empty(L)
       for j in range(L): gc[j]=S*dot(gy[j::L], b)
       gnorm=norm(gc)
       if gnorm small break
       # Adaptive step: step = 0.2 / gnorm? But gnorm units with S; could be 1e-4 -> step 2000 cap 0.3.
       if stuck>200: step *=0.7
       else: step=min(step*1.01,0.5)
       step_use=min(0.5, step)
       x=project_simplex(c_y - step_use*gc,1.0)
       t_new=...
       c_y_x = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-c_y)
       c_y_x=project_simplex(c_y_x,1.0)
       c_y=c_y_x; t=t_new
       y=S*assemble_polyphase(b,c_y)
       if stuck>2000 and it%200==0: noise...
    assemble best_y? best_y stored.
    return best_y, best_c
```
But `run_fista` uses y normalized S; returning best_y. Need `best_obj` for candidate via compute_objective? Since y sum S, best_c equals objective, but use compute to be safe maybe not inside (cost). Return best_y.

### Use of `np.dot` in filter gc loop can be optimized using reshape:
`gy` length n=mL. `gs = gy.reshape(L, m)`? Because index iL+k. If `y[k::L]` same. But reshape in C order: y.reshape(m,L) gives row i elements k. Then `gc = S * (gy.reshape(m,L) * b[:,None]).sum(axis=0)`. This is vectorized. Need shape: y index i*L+k; if reshape(m,L), row i, col k. Then sum over i y[i,k]*b[i] = dot(b, gy[k::L]). #### Imports

#### normalize
```
def normalize_sequence(a, target=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    s=a.sum()
    if target is None:
        target=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, target/n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a*(target/s)
```
If target=1, values sum1.

#### fft_convolve_self
```
def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n=a.size
    size=2*n-1
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    fsize=next_fast_len(size)
    A=rfft(a,fsize)
    c=np.real(irfft(A*A, fsize))
    c[:size] = np.maximum(c[:size], 0.0)? Convolution nonnegative but numerical negatives. Clip? Clipping can slightly change objective but OK. Use small clip to 0 for weights. But if all negative? no.
    return c[:size]
```
Clipping max to nonnegative; if due roundoff negative, okay. But for derivative gradient? We only clip c for weights; objective max not affected if negative small. Use `np.maximum(c[:size], 0.0)`? If some coefficients should be negative? a nonnegative, coefficients nonnegative. Good.

#### compute_objective
```
def compute_objective(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return np.inf
    c=fft_convolve_self(a)
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0*n*cm/(s*s))
```

#### project_simplex
Standard.

#### update_best
```
def update_best(best_a, best_obj, cand, obj=None):
    if cand is None: return best_a,best_obj
    cand=np.asarray(cand,float).reshape(-1)
    if cand.size==0: return best_a,best_obj
    if obj is None:
        obj=compute_objective(cand)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
        return cand.copy(), float(obj)
    return best_a,best_obj
```

#### symmetrize
```
def symmetrize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    b=0.5*(a+a[::-1])
    s=b.sum(); old=a.sum()
    if s>1e-300 and old>0: b=b*(old/s)
    return b
```

#### downsample/upsample
```
def downsample_average(a,target):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
   if target<=0: return np.zeros(0)
   if n<=target:
       return upsample_linear(a,target)
   if n % target ==0:
       factor=n//target
       return a[:target*factor].reshape(target,factor).mean(axis=1)
   # interpolation fallback
   old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
   new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,target,endpoint=False)
   return np.interp(new,old,a)
```
Need upsample_linear defined before? In Python top-level, functions can refer later if defined before call? `downsample_average` calls `upsample_linear`, defined later but at runtime name resolved; okay but define upsample first.

`upsample_linear(a,target)`:

```
old=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); new=...; return np.interp(new,old,a)
```

#### assemble_polyphase
```
def assemble_polyphase(base,c):
   m=base.size; L=c.size; y=np.empty(m*L)
   for k in range(L): y[k::L]=base*c[k]
   return y
```
If L=1 works.

#### make_filter_init
```
def make_filter_init(L):
   c=np.ones(L)
   if L>=2:
      c[L//2]=0.0
   s=c.sum()
   if s<=0: c=np.ones(L)
   return c/c.sum()
```
Maybe for L=2 one zero -> [1,0] bad? We need L=2 pattern not degenerate. For L=2, zero at1 -> [1,0] not good. Handle L==2 return [1/3,2/3]? But factor maybe. Use [1/3,2/3] (sum1). Actually p=1/3 for first? y even = p, odd=1-p; p=1/3 gives factor? For uniform base length2, c=[1/3,2/3] gives objective maybe 20/9; c=[2/3,1/3] symmetric same. Good.
L=3 maybe [1/2,0,1/2]? Could be. Use zero middle. OK.
```
if L==2: c=np.array([1.0/3,2.0/3])
else zero middle
```

#### run_fista
Let's code with rfft.

```
def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=5000.0, step_scale=1.0, topk_max=2048, topk_den=32, use_noise=True):
    if max_time <= 0 or a_init is None: return None, np.inf
    a=normalize_sequence(a_init, None) # S target
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return None, np.inf
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a[:]=a*(S/a.sum()) # normalize already
    fsize=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; beta=float(beta_init)
    c=fft_convolve_self(y)
    best_c=float(c.max()); best=y.copy(); last=0; it=0; step_scale_cur=float(step_scale)
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()-start < max_time:
       it+=1
       beta = min(float(beta_max), beta*(1.0+beta_rate))
       A=rfft(y,fsize)
       cc=np.real(irfft(A*A,fsize)); cc=cc[:2*n-1]
       cmax=float(cc.max())
       if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_c=cmax; best=y.copy(); last=it
       stuck=it-last
       size=2*n-1
       use_top = (beta > 500.0) or (stuck > 800 and ((it % 3)==0 or (rng.random() < 0.2)))
       if use_top:
           k=max(4, min(int(topk_max), max(4, n//topk_den)))
           if stuck > 2000:
               k=min(int(topk_max), int(k * (1.0 + stuck/5000.0)))
           if k >= size:
               vals=cc
               idx=None
           else:
               idx=np.argpartition(cc, size-k)[size-k:]
               vals=cc[idx]
           vmax=float(vals.max())
           top_beta = min(300.0, max(20.0, beta*0.05))
           shift=top_beta*(vals-vmax)
           shift-=shift.max()
           e=np.exp(shift)
           esum=e.sum()
           wvals=e/esum if esum>1e-300 else np.ones_like(vals,dtype=float)/vals.size
           w=np.zeros(size)
           if idx is None: w=wvals
           else: w[idx]=wvals
           beta_eff=min(100.0, max(20.0, beta*0.05))
       else:
           shift=beta*(cc-cmax)
           shift-=shift.max()
           e=np.exp(shift)
           esum=e.sum()
           if esum>1e-300:
               w=e/esum
           else:
               w=np.zeros(size); w[int(np.argmax(cc))]=1.0
           beta_eff=max(1.0,beta)
       if use_noise and stuck > 2500 and (it % 250 == 0):
           noise_scale=0.002*S/np.sqrt(n)*np.exp(-stuck/6000.0)
           if noise_scale > 1e-6:
               y=project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n), S)
               t=1.0
               if beta > 100.0: beta=50.0
       W=rfft(w,fsize)
       g=2.0*np.real(irfft(W*np.conj(A), fsize))[:n]
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-15: break
       step=step_scale_cur / max(beta_eff,1.0)
       if stuck > 500:
           step *= max(0.25, 500.0/(500.0+stuck))
           step_scale_cur=max(0.3, step_scale_cur*0.985)
       else:
           step_scale_cur=min(1.5, step_scale_cur*1.001)
       x=project_simplex(y - step*g, S)
       if x.sum() < 1e-15:
           x=np.full(n, S/n)
       t_new=0.5*(1.0+np.sqrt(1.0+4.0*t*t))
       y=x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
       ys=float(y.sum())
       if ys < 1e-15:
           y=best.copy()
       else:
           y *= S/ys
       a=x
       t=t_new
    # recompute best? maybe
    return best, best_c
```
Potential issue: `rng.random()` attribute; if rng is np.random.Generator yes. Good.
If `topk_max` int and `n//topk_den` maybe 0 for small n; max4.
When use_top, after adding noise we still compute gradient using old A? We add noise before W and g, but A was computed from old y. If we perturb y, A stale. Better noise after update? In code above, noise before gradient but A from old y. Could update if noise: recompute A if noise applied. Simpler move noise after update at end. But not critical. Let's move noise to after `a=x; t=t_new`:
```
if use_noise and stuck>...:
   y=project(...); t=1; if beta>100: beta=50
```
Then A next iter. We'll do.

Also if noise applied, `a` old x; y maybe not equal x; okay.

Return best_c objective. If `best` sum S. Good.

#### optimize_filter_for_base
```
def optimize_filter_for_base(base, L, max_time, rng, init_c=None, beta=20.0):
   if max_time is None or max_time <=0:
       b=normalize_sequence(base,1.0); c=...; y=S*assemble; return y, compute? return maxc
   m=base.size; n=m*L; S=np.sqrt(2*n)
   b=normalize_sequence(base,1.0)
   c0=init_c if not None else make_filter_init(L)
   c=normalize_sequence(c0,1.0)
   c_y=c.copy(); t=1.0
   fsize=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   y=S*assemble_polyphase(b,c_y); y = normalize? sum S.
   conv=fft_convolve_self(y); best_c=conv.max(); best_y=y.copy(); last=0; it=0; step=0.1; start=...
   while time < max_time:
      it+=1
      A=rfft(y,fsize); cc=...; cmax...
      if cmax<best_c... last
      stuck=it-last
      # weights full LSE with beta; if stuck large use topk k=32
      if stuck>500 and (it%5==0):
         k=min(64, size//32? maybe 32)
         idx top; vals; shift=50*(vals-vmax); ... w
      else:
         shift=beta*(cc-cmax); ... if underflow top1
      gy=2*real(irfft(rfft(w,fsize)*conj(A),fsize))[:n]
      gc=S*(np.dot(b, gy.reshape(m,L))) # check reshape
      gnorm=norm(gc)
      if gnorm<1e-15: break
      step_use=min(0.3, max(1e-6, 0.1/(gnorm+1e-15)))
      if stuck>500: step_use*=max(0.2,500/(500+stuck))
      x=project_simplex(c_y - step_use*gc,1.0)
      if x.sum()<1e-15: x=np.full(L,1/L)
      t_new=...
      c_y = x + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x-c_y)
      c_y=project_simplex(c_y,1.0)
      if stuck>2000 and it%100==0: noise...
      t=t_new
      y=S*assemble_polyphase(b,c_y)
   return best_y,best_c
```
Check `gy.reshape(m,L)` layout: y length m*L generated by `y[k::L]=base*c[k]`. This is C order index i*L + k. reshape(m,L) gives row i, cols k. Good. `np.dot(b, gy.reshape(m,L))` returns (L,) = ∑ b_i gy_{i,k}. Good.
If L large and m small? okay.

If `base` size 0, return. If m*L maybe 1, reshape works.

#### lp_polish
Implement with loops? Top-level.
```
def lp_polish(a, max_time, max_rows=64):
   if linprog is None or a is None or max_time<=0: return a
   n=len(a); if n==0 or n>65536: return a
   S=np.sqrt(2*n); a=normalize_sequence(a,S)
   c=fft_convolve_self(a); M=float(c.max())
   size=2*n-1
   r=min(max_rows, size)
   # include top and maybe argmax; top.
   if r <= size:
      active = np.argpartition(c, size-r)[size-r:]
   else active all?
   # ensure size-r valid if r=0
   A=np.zeros((r,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for t,k in enumerate(active):
      j0=max(0,int(k-n+1)); j1=min(n-1,int(k)); length=j1-j0+1
      if length>0:
         A[t,j0:j1+1] = a[int(k-j1):int(k-j0+1)][::-1]
   U=float(max(0.05, 10.0*S/n, 2.0*float(np.max(a))))
   cvec=-np.ones(n)
   try:
      sol=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(r,float(M)), bounds=[(0.0,U)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
   except Exception:
      try:
         sol=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(r,float(M)), bounds=[(0.0,U)]*n)
      except Exception:
         return a
   if not getattr(sol,'success',False) or getattr(sol,'x',None) is None: return a
   b=np.asarray(sol.x,float)
   s=float(b.sum())
   if s<=1e-12: return a
   b_scaled=b*(S/s)
   best=a; best_obj=compute_objective(a)
   for frac in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
      cand=(1-frac)*a + frac*b_scaled
      cs=cand.sum()
      if cs>1e-15:
         cand=cand*(S/cs)
         obj=compute_objective(cand)
         if obj<best_obj: best=cand; best_obj=obj
   return best
```
Could be slow building list bounds. If n16k ok. If n32k ok. If r=64. A rows maybe many zeros. If M inf? no.
If `c` nan? no.

#### Random smooth fallback
```
def fallback_sequence(n, rng=None):
   # edge loaded cos
   i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   x=i/n
   a=1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x) + 0.25*(2*x-1)**2? 
   a=np.clip(a,1e-3, None)
   return normalize_sequence(a, None)
```

#### propose_candidate

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,total-45.0)
    best_a=None; best_obj=np.inf
    par=get_parent_sequence()
    if par is not None:
       par=normalize_sequence(par, None)
       best_a,best_obj=update_best(None,np.inf,par)
    else:
       best_a=fallback_sequence(8192); best_obj=compute_objective(best_a)
    print initial
    # helper update? We'll use update_best.
```
Need top-level `get_parent_sequence`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
   seq=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
   if seq is None: return None
   try:
      arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
      arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
      if arr.size==0 or arr.sum()<1e-8: return None
      return arr
   except Exception: return None
```

Phase1:
```
rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
```

### `multiscale_to_target` code details
```
def multiscale_to_target(base, target_n, max_time, rng):
    local_start=time.monotonic(); local_deadline=local_start+max_time
    best=None; best_obj=np.inf
    blen=len(base)
    configs=[]
    for L in (4,8,2,16,32):
        if target_n % L==0:
            m=target_n//L
            if m <= blen and m >0:
                configs.append((L,m))
    # Also if L=1? just base target? If target==blen.
    if target_n == blen:
       # short fista maybe
       if max_time>0:
          a,_=run_fista(base,min(30,max_time),rng,beta_init=20, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=1000, step_scale=1.0)
          return a, ...
    per = max(20, min(80, (local_deadline-local_start)/max(1,len(configs)*3)))
    for L,m in configs:
       rem=local_deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem < 30: break
       base_m=downsample_average(base,m)
       fb=min(per, rem*0.45)
       if fb <=5: continue
       init=make_filter_init(L)
       y,objc=optimize_filter_for_base(base_m,L,fb,rng,init_c=init)
       best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,y,objc)
       rem=local_deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem > 25:
          sb=min(per*0.6, rem*0.45)
          y2,_=run_fista(y, sb, rng, beta_init=20, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=2000, step_scale=1.0)
          best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,y2)
    rem=local_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem>40 and target_n <= 4*blen:
       y=upsample_linear(base,target_n)
       best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,y)
       sb=min(40, rem*0.5)
       y2,_=run_fista(y,sb,rng,beta_init=20,beta_rate=0.004,beta_max=2000,step_scale=1.0)
       best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,y2)
    return best,best_obj
```

### Fallback sequence
Let's design deterministic robust initial:
```
def fallback_sequence(n, rng=None):
   i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
   x=i/(n-1 if n>1 else 1)
   a=1.0 + 0.35*np.cos(2*np.pi*x) + 0.25*np.abs(2*x-1.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
   return normalize_sequence(a, None)
```
Maybe better edge loaded: a=1 + 0.5*(2x-1)^2 + 0.3 cos(4πx). Use clip. Fine.

### Specific code snippets

`get_parent_sequence`:
```
def get_parent_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None: return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0: return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-8 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    return arr
```

`safe_final_list`:
```
def safe_final_list(a):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
       arr=None
    if arr is None or arr.size==0:
       arr=fallback_sequence(8192)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-8:
       arr=fallback_sequence(8192)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
       arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s<1e-8: arr=fallback_sequence(8192)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```

### Maybe `np.nan_to_num` with posinf? If input inf, clip already but if nan? ok.

```
def multiscale_to_target(base, target_n, max_time, rng):
    if base is None: return None, np.inf
    base=normalize_sequence(base,None)
    blen=base.size
    if blen==0: return None, np.inf
    local_deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    best=None; best_obj=np.inf
    if target_n <= blen:

        best,best_obj=update_best(best,best_obj,base_t)
        if time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
           a_t,_=run_fista(base_t, min(60.0, local_deadline-time.monotonic()+1?), rng,...)
           best,best_obj=update_best(...)
        return best,best_obj
    configs=[]...
```

```

if rem>20:
   sb=min(per*0.5, rem*0.45)
   y2,_=run_fista(y,sb,...)
   update
```
`per` could be e.g. 80; fb80, sb maybe40. Total configs maybe 4, time limited.

```python
"""
Multi-scale polyphase initialization + FISTA smoothing + active-set LP polishing.

Minimize 2*n*max(a*a)/(sum a)^2 over non-negative sequences.
The code treats the problem as minimizing max convolution under a fixed
L1 norm, builds refined candidates by polyphase up-sampling from coarser
solutions, then polishes them with log-sum-exp FISTA, symmetrization,
and a small active-set linear program based on f*b <= max(f*f).
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def get_parent_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-8:
        return None
    return arr


def normalize_sequence(a, target=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if target is None:
        target = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, target / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    S = float(S)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_arr = (css - S) / idx
    valid = u > theta_arr

    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.nonzero(valid)[0][-1]) + 1
        theta = float((css[rho - 1] - S) / rho)
    else:
        theta = float((float(v.sum()) - S) / n)

    out = v - theta
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def fft_convolve_self(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fsize = int(next_fast_len(size))
    A = rfft(a, fsize)
    c = np.real(irfft(A * A, fsize))
    c = c[:size]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def compute_objective(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (s * s))


def update_best(best_a, best_obj, cand, obj=None):
    if cand is None:
        return best_a, best_obj
    cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if cand.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
        return best_a, best_obj
    if obj is None:
        obj = compute_objective(cand)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
        return cand.copy(), float(obj)
    return best_a, best_obj


def symmetrize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    old_s = float(a.sum())
    b = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s > 1e-15 and old_s > 1e-15:
        b = b * (old_s / s)
    return b


def upsample_linear(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    target = int(target)
    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(target, dtype=np.float64)
    if target == n:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(new, old, a).astype(np.float64)


def downsample_average(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    target = int(target)
    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(target, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= target:
        return upsample_linear(a, target)
    if n % target == 0:
        factor = n // target
        return a[:target * factor].reshape(target, factor).mean(axis=1)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, target, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(new, old, a).astype(np.float64)


def assemble_polyphase(base, c):
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    m = base.size
    L = c.size
    if m == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.empty(m * L, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(L):
        y[k::L] = base * c[k]
    return y


def make_filter_init(L):
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 2:
        return np.array([1.0 / 3.0, 2.0 / 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    c[L // 2] = 0.0
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        c = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(L)
    return c / s


def fallback_sequence(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([np.sqrt(2.0)], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = 1.0 + 0.45 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2 + 0.25 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
    return normalize_sequence(a, None)


def run_fista(a_init, max_time, rng,
              beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004, beta_max=5000.0,
              step_scale=1.0, topk_max=2048, topk_den=32,
              use_noise=True):
    if a_init is None or max_time is None or max_time <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng()

    a = normalize_sequence(a_init, None)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = a * (S / max(1e-300, float(a.sum())))

    fsize = int(next_fast_len(2 * n - 1))
    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    beta = float(beta_init)
    size = 2 * n - 1

    c0 = fft_convolve_self(y)
    best_c = float(np.max(c0))
    best = y.copy()
    last_improve = 0
    it = 0
    step_scale_cur = float(step_scale)
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        beta = min(float(beta_max), beta * (1.0 + float(beta_rate)))

        A = rfft(y, fsize)
        cc = np.real(irfft(A * A, fsize))[:size]
        cmax = float(np.max(cc))

        if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cmax
            best = y.copy()
            last_improve = it
        stuck = it - last_improve

        use_top = (beta > 500.0) or (stuck > 800 and ((it % 3) == 0 or (rng.random() < 0.2)))
        if use_top:
            k = max(4, min(int(topk_max), max(4, n // max(1, topk_den))))
            if stuck > 2000:
                k = min(int(topk_max), int(k * (1.0 + stuck / 5000.0)))
            if k >= size:
                vals = cc
                idx = None
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(cc, size - k)[size - k:]
                vals = cc[idx]
            vmax = float(np.max(vals))
            top_beta = min(300.0, max(20.0, beta * 0.05))
            shift = top_beta * (vals - vmax)
            shift -= float(np.max(shift))
            e = np.exp(shift)
            es = float(e.sum())
            if es > 1e-300:
                wvals = e / es
            else:
                wvals = np.ones_like(vals, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, vals.size)
            w = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
            if idx is None:
                w = wvals
            else:
                w[idx] = wvals
            beta_eff = min(100.0, max(20.0, beta * 0.05))
        else:
            shift = beta * (cc - cmax)
            shift -= float(np.max(shift))
            e = np.exp(shift)
            es = float(e.sum())
            if es > 1e-300:
                w = e / es
            else:
                w = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(cc))] = 1.0
            beta_eff = max(1.0, beta)

        W = rfft(w, fsize)
        g = 2.0 * np.real(irfft(W * np.conj(A), fsize))[:n]
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        step = step_scale_cur / max(beta_eff, 1.0)
        if stuck > 500:
            step *= max(0.25, 500.0 / (500.0 + stuck))
            step_scale_cur = max(0.3, step_scale_cur * 0.985)
        else:
            step_scale_cur = min(1.5, step_scale_cur * 1.001)

        x = project_simplex(y - step * g, S)
        xs = float(x.sum())
        if xs < 1e-15:
            x = np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        ys = float(y.sum())
        if ys < 1e-15:
            y = best.copy()
        else:
            y = y * (S / ys)

        a = x
        t = t_new

        if use_noise and stuck > 2500 and (it % 250 == 0):
            noise_scale = 0.002 * S / np.sqrt(n) * np.exp(-stuck / 6000.0)
            if noise_scale > 1e-6:
                y = project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n), S)
                t = 1.0
                if beta > 100.0:
                    beta = 50.0

    return best, best_c


def optimize_filter_for_base(base, L, max_time, rng, init_c=None, beta=20.0):
    if base is None or L is None or int(L) <= 0 or max_time is None or max_time <= 0.0:
        if base is None or int(L) <= 0:
            return None, np.inf
        b = normalize_sequence(base, 1.0)
        c = normalize_sequence(init_c if init_c is not None else make_filter_init(int(L)), 1.0)
        Sd = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * b.size * int(L)))
        y = Sd * assemble_polyphase(b, c)
        return y, compute_objective(y)

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng()

    L = int(L)
    b = normalize_sequence(base, 1.0)
    m = b.size
    if m == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    c0 = init_c if init_c is not None else make_filter_init(L)
    c = normalize_sequence(c0, 1.0)
    y_c = c.copy()
    t = 1.0
    n = m * L
    Sd = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    fsize = int(next_fast_len(2 * n - 1))
    size = 2 * n - 1

    y = Sd * assemble_polyphase(b, y_c)
    c0 = fft_convolve_self(y)
    best_c = float(np.max(c0))
    best_y = y.copy()
    last_improve = 0
    it = 0
    step = 0.15
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < max_time:
        it += 1
        A = rfft(y, fsize)
        cc = np.real(irfft(A * A, fsize))[:size]
        cmax = float(np.max(cc))

        if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cmax
            best_y = y.copy()
            last_improve = it
        stuck = it - last_improve

        use_top = (stuck > 500 and (it % 5) == 0)
        if use_top:
            k = min(64, max(8, size // 256))
            if k >= size:
                vals = cc
                idx = None
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(cc, size - k)[size - k:]
                vals = cc[idx]
            vals_max = float(np.max(vals))
            shift = 50.0 * (vals - vals_max)
            shift -= float(np.max(shift))
            e = np.exp(shift)
            es = float(e.sum())
            wvals = e / es if es > 1e-300 else np.ones_like(vals, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, vals.size)
            w = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
            if idx is None:
                w = wvals
            else:
                w[idx] = wvals
        else:
            shift = beta * (cc - cmax)
            shift -= float(np.max(shift))
            e = np.exp(shift)
            es = float(e.sum())
            if es > 1e-300:
                w = e / es
            else:
                w = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(cc))] = 1.0

        W = rfft(w, fsize)
        gy = 2.0 * np.real(irfft(W * np.conj(A), fsize))[:n]
        gc = Sd * (np.dot(b, gy.reshape(m, L)))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gc))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        step_use = min(0.30, max(1e-6, 0.1 / (gnorm + 1e-15)))
        if stuck > 500:
            step_use *= max(0.2, 500.0 / (500.0 + stuck))

        x = project_simplex(y_c - step_use * gc, 1.0)
        xs = float(x.sum())
        if xs < 1e-15:
            x = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y_c = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - y_c)
        y_c = project_simplex(y_c, 1.0)
        t = t_new

        if stuck > 2000 and (it % 100) == 0:
            y_c = project_simplex(y_c + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, L), 1.0)
            t = 1.0

        y = Sd * assemble_polyphase(b, y_c)

    return best_y, best_c


def multiscale_to_target(base, target_n, max_time, rng):
    if base is None or target_n is None or int(target_n) <= 0:
        return None, np.inf
    if max_time is None or max_time <= 0.0:
        base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        t = int(target_n)
        b = downsample_average(base, t)
        return b, compute_objective(b)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng()

    target_n = int(target_n)
    base = normalize_sequence(base, None)
    blen = base.size
    if blen == 0:
        return None, np.inf

    local_deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_time)
    best = None
    best_obj = np.inf

    if target_n <= blen:
        base_t = downsample_average(base, target_n)
        best, best_obj = update_best(best, best_obj, base_t)
        rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 0:
            a_t, _ = run_fista(base_t, min(90.0, rem), rng,
                               beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004,
                               beta_max=3000.0, step_scale=1.0)
            best, best_obj = update_best(best, best_obj, a_t)
        return best, best_obj

    configs = []
    for L in (4, 8, 2, 16, 32):
        if target_n % L == 0:
            m = target_n // L
            if 0 < m <= blen:
                configs.append((L, m))

    ncfg = max(1, len(configs))
    per = max(20.0, min(90.0, float(max_time) / (3.0 * ncfg)))

    for L, m in configs:
        rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        base_m = downsample_average(base, m)
        c0 = make_filter_init(L)
        fb = min(per, 0.45 * rem)
        if fb > 5.0:
            y, objc = optimize_filter_for_base(base_m, L, fb, rng, init_c=c0)
            best, best_obj = update_best(best, best_obj, y, objc)
        rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 25.0:
            sb = min(0.55 * per, 0.45 * rem)
            y2, _ = run_fista(y, sb, rng,
                              beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004,
                              beta_max=3000.0, step_scale=1.0)
            best, best_obj = update_best(best, best_obj, y2)

    rem = local_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0 and target_n <= 4 * blen:
        yl = upsample_linear(base, target_n)
        best, best_obj = update_best(best, best_obj, yl)
        sb = min(45.0, 0.5 * rem)
        y2, _ = run_fista(yl, sb, rng,
                          beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.004,
                          beta_max=3000.0, step_scale=1.0)
        best, best_obj = update_best(best, best_obj, y2)

    return best, best_obj


def lp_polish(a, max_time, max_rows=64):
    if a is None or max_time is None or max_time <= 0.0 or linprog is None:
        return a
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0 or n > 65536:
        return a

    a_cur = normalize_sequence(a, None)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * r)) if (r := n) > 0 else 0.0
    a_cur = normalize_sequence(a_cur, S)
    best = a_cur.copy()
    best_obj = compute_objective(best)
    r_lp = int(max_rows)
    n_iter = 2 if n <= 8192 else 1

    for _ in range(max(1, n_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() + 5.0 > time.monotonic() + max(0.0, 0.0):
            pass
        c = fft_convolve_self(a_cur)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        size = 2 * n - 1
        r = min(r_lp, size)
        if r <= 0:
            break
        if r >= size:
            active = np.arange(size)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, size - r)[size - r:]

        A = np.zeros((r, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for row, k in enumerate(active):
            k = int(k)
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(n - 1, k)
            length = j1 - j0 + 1
            if length > 0:
                A[row, j0:j1 + 1] = a_cur[k - j1:k - j0 + 1][::-1]

        U = float(max(0.05, 10.0 * S / n, 2.0 * float(np.max(a_cur))))
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

        try:
            sol = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(r, float(M)),
                          bounds=[(0.0, U)] * n, method="highs",
                          options={"presolve": True})
        except Exception:
            try:
                sol = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(r, float(M)),
                              bounds=[(0.0, U)] * n)
            except Exception:
                break

        if not getattr(sol, "success", False) or getattr(sol, "x", None) is None:
            break

        b = np.asarray(sol.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        bs = float(b.sum())
        if bs <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(bs):
            break
        b_s = b * (S / bs)

        for frac in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - frac) * a_cur + frac * b_s
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs <= 1e-15:
                continue
            cand = cand * (S / cs)
            obj = compute_objective(cand)
            if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_obj = obj
                a_cur = cand.copy()

    return best


def safe_final_list(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        arr = None
    if arr is None or arr.size == 0:
        arr = fallback_sequence(8192)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-8:
        arr = fallback_sequence(8192)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, total - 45.0)

    best_a = None
    best_obj = np.inf

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is not None:
        parent = normalize_sequence(parent, None)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(None, np.inf, parent)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = fallback_sequence(8192)
        best_obj = compute_objective(best_a)

    print("Initial: n=%d obj=%.6f" % (len(best_a), best_obj))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0 and len(best_a) <= 32768:
        p1 = min(150.0, 0.08 * rem)
        a1, _ = run_fista(best_a, p1, rng,
                          beta_init=15.0, beta_rate=0.005,
                          beta_max=3000.0, step_scale=1.0)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a1)
        print("Phase1: n=%d obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (len(best_a), best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

    target1 = 16384
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) < target1 and rem > 250.0:
        ms_time = min(650.0, 0.38 * rem)
        cand16, _ = multiscale_to_target(best_a, target1, ms_time, rng)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, cand16)
        print("Phase2a multi-scale 16384: obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if cand16 is not None and rem > 120.0:
            ft = min(900.0, 0.45 * rem)
            a2, _ = run_fista(cand16, ft, rng,
                              beta_init=10.0, beta_rate=0.003,
                              beta_max=4000.0, step_scale=1.0)
            best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a2)
            print("Phase2b FISTA 16384: obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 90.0:
                ft2 = min(300.0, 0.30 * rem)
                a3, _ = run_fista(best_a, ft2, rng,
                                  beta_init=100.0, beta_rate=0.001,
                                  beta_max=20000.0, step_scale=0.4)
                best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a3)
                print("Phase2c polish 16384: obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

    target2 = 32768
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) < target2 and rem > 450.0:
        ms_time = min(260.0, 0.35 * rem)
        cand32, _ = multiscale_to_target(best_a, target2, ms_time, rng)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, cand32)
        print("Phase3a multi-scale 32768: obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if cand32 is not None and rem > 90.0:
            ft = min(350.0, 0.40 * rem)
            a4, _ = run_fista(cand32, ft, rng,
                              beta_init=20.0, beta_rate=0.002,
                              beta_max=5000.0, step_scale=0.8)
            best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a4)
            print("Phase3b FISTA 32768: obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

    if best_a is not None:
        sy = symmetrize(best_a)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, sy)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and rem > 80.0 and len(best_a) <= 32768:
        ft = min(450.0, 0.25 * rem)
        a5, _ = run_fista(best_a, ft, rng,
                          beta_init=300.0, beta_rate=0.0008,
                          beta_max=50000.0, step_scale=0.3)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a5)
        print("Phase4 high-beta polish: obj=%.6f elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, time.monotonic() - t0))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and 0 < len(best_a) <= 32768 and rem > 70.0:
        nb = len(best_a)
        max_rows = 128 if nb <= 2048 else (64 if nb <= 16384 else 32)
        lp_time = min(120.0, 0.22 * rem)
        lp_cand = lp_polish(best_a, lp_time, max_rows=max_rows)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, lp_cand)
        print("Phase5 LP polish: obj=%.6f n=%d elapsed=%.0fs" % (best_obj, len(best_a), time.monotonic() - t0))

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 35.0:
            ft = min(70.0, 0.15 * rem)
            a6, _ = run_fista(best_a, ft, rng,
                              beta_init=80.0, beta_rate=0.002,
                              beta_max=10000.0, step_scale=0.5)
            best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a6)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and rem > 50.0:
        nb = len(best_a)
        S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * nb))
        noise = 0.003 * S / np.sqrt(nb)
        pert = project_simplex(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, noise, nb), S)
        a7, _ = run_fista(pert, min(45.0, 0.15 * rem), rng,
                          beta_init=50.0, beta_rate=0.004,
                          beta_max=5000.0, step_scale=1.0)
        best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, a7)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = fallback_sequence(8192)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    while len(best_a) > 32768:
        best_a = downsample_average(best_a, len(best_a) // 2)

    if len(best_a) > 16384:
        ds16 = downsample_average(best_a, 16384)
        obj16 = compute_objective(ds16)
        obj32 = compute_objective(best_a)
        if np.isfinite(obj16) and (not np.isfinite(obj32) or obj16 <= obj32 + 0.005):
            best_a = ds16
            best_obj = obj16

    for _ in range(2):
        if len(best_a) > 4096:
            ds = downsample_average(best_a, len(best_a) // 2)
            best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, ds)

    best_a = symmetrize(best_a)
    best_a, best_obj = update_best(best_a, best_obj, best_a)

    final_list = safe_final_list(best_a)
    try:
        verified = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if not np.isfinite(verified):
            final_list = safe_final_list(fallback_sequence(8192))
            verified = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        print("Final verified objective: %.6f n=%d" % (verified, len(final_list)))
    except Exception:
        # If evaluation is unexpectedly unavailable/invalid, return a safe fallback.
        final_list = safe_final_list(fallback_sequence(8192))

    print("Total elapsed: %.0fs" % (time.monotonic() - t0))
    return final_list
```
